```json
  "sign_method": "HANDWRITTEN",
  "procedure_risk_acknowledged": true,
  "alternative_acknowledged": true,
  "proxy_flag": false,
  "proxy_authorization_url": null,
  "witness_relation": null,
  "guardian_relation": null,
  "face_photo_url": "/upload/m11/face/TYS20261031000013.jpg"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "consent_no": "TYS20261031000013",
    "sign_status": "SIGNING",
    "signed_count": 1,
    "required_signers": 2,
    "remaining_signers": ["DOCTOR"],
    "eye_side_confirmed": "OS"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验同意书存在且状态 `PENDING`/`SIGNING` → `"1105009"`；2) **患者签署时必须勾选 `procedure_risk_acknowledged` 与 `alternative_acknowledged`** → `"1105029"`；3) 代理签署必须有 `proxy_authorization_url` → `"1105029"`；4) 见证人必须有 `witness_relation`，监护人必须有 `guardian_relation`；5) **校验 `eye_side_confirmed` 与预约眼别一致**（防生成后被改）→ `"1105012"`；6) 按 `signer_role` 写入对应签名列与时间；7) `signed_count` 递增；8) 达到 `required_signers` 且患者确认项齐备 → `sign_status = 'SIGNED'`，写 `valid_from = now`；9) 患者签署时记录 `patient_sign_ip`、`patient_sign_device`、`patient_face_url`；10) 若所有同意书均 `SIGNED`，更新主表 `consent_completed = true`；11) 写日志 `CONSENT_SIGN`。

**错误码：** `"1105009"`、`"1105029"`、`"1105012"`、`40001`。

**响应**

```json
    "consent_no": "TYS20261031000013",

    "revoked_at": "2026-10-31T17:00:00",

  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验同意书存在且状态为 `SIGNED`/`SIGNING` → `"1105009"`；2) `revoke_reason` ≥4 字 → `"1105031"`；3) 状态置 `REVOKED`，写 `revoked_by`、`revoked_at`、原因；4) 更新主表 `consent_completed = false`；5) **若预约处于 `PREPARING`/`READY`，将状态回退为 `CONFIRMED`**（同意书失效意味着准备不再齐备）；6) 写日志 `CONSENT_REVOKE`（`is_safety_related = TRUE`）。

**错误码：** `"1105009"`、`"1105031"`、`"1105001"`。

#### API-25 防篡改校验

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005/consents/TYS20261031000013/verify
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "consent_no": "TYS20261031000013",
    "stored_hash": "a3f2b8c91d4e7f6a2b5c8d1e4f7a0b3c6d9e2f5a8b1c4d7e0f3a6b9c2d5e8f1a",
    "computed_hash": "a3f2b8c91d4e7f6a2b5c8d1e4f7a0b3c6d9e2f5a8b1c4d7e0f3a6b9c2d5e8f1a",
    "tamper_passed": true,
    "checked_at": "2026-10-31T17:05:00",
    "verified_by": 4001
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 取 `content_snapshot` 重新计算 SHA-256 → `computed_hash`；2) 与库中 `content_hash` 比对；3) 一致则 `tamper_passed = true`；不一致则 `false` **且返回 `"1105030"`**，同时写日志 `CONSENT_TAMPER_CHECK`（`is_safety_related = TRUE`, `result = 'FAILED'`），并告警法务与信息科；4) 更新 `tamper_checked_at`、`tamper_passed`；5) 该接口由定时任务每日全量调用一次，也支持人工触发。

**错误码：** `"1105030"`、`"1105001"`。

**请求**


```json
{
  "check_round": "TIME_OUT",
  "eye_side_verified": "OS",
  "seven_items": {
    "name": true,
    "gender": true,
    "age": true,
    "id_card": true,
    "operation": true,
    "eye_side": true,
    "anesthesia": true
  },
  "identity_method": "WRISTBAND",
  "wristband_no": "WB20261031005",
  "patient_name_confirmed": "张先生",
  "patient_birth_confirmed": "1998-05-12",
  "laterality_mark_verified": true,
  "consent_verified": true,
  "medication_verified": true,
  "education_verified": true,
  "fasting_verified": false,
  "lab_result_verified": true,
  "implant_verified": false,
  "equipment_qualified": true,
  "preop_photo_taken": true,
  "jewelry_removed": true,
  "makeup_removed": true,
  "participant_ids": [2001, 3012, 4001],
  "surgeon_present": true,
  "patient_response_ok": true,
  "signature_url": "/upload/m11/check/8823.png",
  "photo_url": "/upload/m11/check/8823.jpg",
  "remark": null
}
```

**响应（通过）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "check_id": 8823,
    "check_round": "TIME_OUT",
    "check_result": "PASSED",
    "eye_side": {
      "reservation": "OS",
      "plan": "OS",
      "consent": "OS",
      "verified": "OS",
      "match": true,
      "mismatched_sources": []
    },
    "seven_items_all_passed": true,
    "reservation_status_transitioned": true,
    "new_status": "READY",
    "ready_progress": 4
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**响应（眼别不一致 → 硬阻断）**

```json
{
  "code": "1105012",
  "message": "眼别不一致，核对已强制阻断",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "check_id": 8824,
    "check_result": "BLOCKED",
    "eye_side": {
      "reservation": "OD",
      "plan": "OS",
      "consent": "OD",
      "verified": "OS",
      "match": false,
      "mismatched_sources": ["reservation", "consent"]
    },
    "blocked_reason": "眼别四处不一致：预约 OD / 方案 OS / 同意书 OD / 实核 OS",
    "alerted_to": ["surgeon", "director", "quality"],
    "reservation_locked": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑（核心安全接口）：**

1. 校验预约存在且状态在 `CONFIRMED`~`READY` → `"1105001"`/`"1105009"`。
2. 取四个眼别来源：`A = reservation.eye_side`、`B = reservation.eye_side_plan_snap`、`C = consent.eye_side_confirmed`（该预约最近一份 `SIGNED` 同意书）、`D = 请求的 eye_side_verified`。
3. **计算 `eye_side_match`**（见 3.4（4）算法）：
   - `match = (A == D) and (B is None or B == A) and (C is None or C == A)`
4. **若 `match = False` → 强制 `check_result = 'BLOCKED'`**（不是 `FAILED`）：
   - 写入 `eye_side_mismatch_detail`（含 `mismatched_sources`）；
   - 预约状态**不推进**，且若当前为 `READY` 则回退 `PREPARING`；
   - 推送高优先级告警至主刀、科主任、质控科（M03-05 + 站内消息）；
   - 写日志 `EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK`（`is_safety_related = TRUE`）；
   - **返回 `"1105012"`**，HTTP 422。
5. 若 `match = True`：
   - 校验七对全部通过，否则 `check_result = 'FAILED'` → `"1105013"`；
   - 校验必备核实项（`identity_verified`、`consent_verified`、`medication_verified`、`education_verified`、`laterality_mark_verified`），任一为 false → `FAILED` → `"1105013"`；
   - `TIME_OUT` 轮次额外校验 `surgeon_present = true` 且 `len(participant_ids) >= 3` → `"1105013"`；
   - `OR_ENTRY` 轮次额外校验 `handover_to_id` 非空且不等于当前用户 → `"1105013"`；
   - 全部通过 → `check_result = 'PASSED'`。
6. 更新主表 `checkin_completed`（三轮全 `PASSED` 才为 true）。
7. **若四项准备全部完成且最近核对 `eye_side_match = true`，自动将预约置为 `READY`**，写 `ready_at` 与状态日志。
8. `check_seq` 自动计算（同轮次已有记录则 +1）。

**错误码：** `"1105001"`、`"1105009"`、`"1105012"`、`"1105013"`、`40001`。

#### API-28 解除核对硬阻断

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005/checklists/8824/unblock
```

```json
{
  "unblock_approval_no": "AP202611010003",
  "unblock_reason": "预约录入错误，方案与患者确认均为左眼，同意书需重签",
  "confirmed_eye_side": "OS",
  "confirm_by": 2002
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "check_id": 8824,
    "unblocked": true,
    "unblocked_at": "2026-11-01T08:05:00",
    "confirmed_eye_side": "OS",
    "reservation_eye_side_updated": true,
    "new_reservation_eye_side": "OS",
    "consent_revoked": true,
    "recheck_required": true,
    "reservation_status": "PREPARING"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验核对记录存在且 `check_result = 'BLOCKED'` 且未解除 → `"1105009"`；2) `unblock_approval_no` 必填 → `"1105032"`；3) `unblock_reason` ≥4 字；4) 调 M20 校验审批单（降级为记录待补验）；5) `confirm_by` 必须是科主任或主刀（校验角色）；6) **若 `confirmed_eye_side` 与当前 `reservation.eye_side` 不同，则同步更新预约眼别**（这是解除阻断的实质动作）；7) 若因此眼别变化，**撤销原同意书并要求重签**；8) 写 `unblock_*` 字段；9) 生成一条新的核对记录（`check_seq + 1`，`PENDING`）要求重新核对；10) 写日志 `CHECKLIST_UNBLOCK`（`is_safety_related = TRUE`，含审批单号）；11) 预约状态保持 `PREPARING`，需重新核对通过才能进 `READY`。

**错误码：** `"1105032"`、`"1105009"`、`"1105012"`、`"119001"`。

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验预约状态在 `DRAFT`~`READY` → `"1105009"`；乐观锁 → `"1105019"`；2) `reason_detail` ≥5 字 → `"1105018"`；3) **判断是否需要审批**：若手术时间距今 < `CANCEL_APPROVAL_HOURS`（24 小时）→ `approval_required = true`，缺 `approval_no` 则 `"1105033"`；4) 状态置 `CANCELLED`，写 `cancel_reason`、`cancel_reason_code`、`cancelled_by`、`cancelled_at`；5) 写 `m11_reservation_change`（`change_type = 'CANCEL'`，`change_seq` 递增）；6) **推送 M06 取消**（`m06_cancel_pushed`，失败则异步重试 → `"1105021"`）；7) 若已缴费则生成 M16-08 退款申请，金额由 M16 计算后回写 `refund_amount_snap`（**本模块不计算金额**）；8) 通知患者（M03-05）；9) 写状态日志 `CANCEL` 与审计日志。

**错误码：** `"1105009"`、`"1105019"`、`"1105018"`、`"1105033"`、`"1105021"`。

#### API-30 改期（生成新预约）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005/postpone
Idempotency-Key: 9e2b7c4a-1f8d-4a3b-8e6c-5d1f9b3a7c2e
```

```json
{
  "new_surgery_date": "2026-11-05",
  "new_surgeon_id": 2001,
  "reason_code": "PATIENT_REQUEST",
  "reason_detail": "患者临时出差，申请延后一周",
  "is_hospital_side": false,
  "approval_no": null,
  "notify_patient": true,
  "version": 4
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "original_reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "new_reservation_no": "YY202610310012",
    "old_status": "PREPARING",
    "new_status": "POSTPONED",
    "change_no": "BG202610310002",
    "change_seq": 1,
    "delay_days": 4,
    "new_surgery_date": "2026-11-05",
    "consent_still_valid": true,
    "exam_still_valid": true,
    "new_consent_required": false,
    "patient_notified": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态在 `DRAFT`~`READY` → `"1105009"`；2) **校验 `new_surgery_date > original_surgery_date`** → `"1105034"`；3) 校验改期次数 < `CHANGE_MAX_TIMES`（3 次）→ `"1105035"`；4) 校验术前检查有效期：新日期是否仍在检查有效期内，超期则 `"1105036"`（或标记 `new_consent_required` 与需重做检查提示）；5) **生成新预约**（`reservation_source = 'RESCHEDULE'`，`parent_reservation_no` 指向原预约，`change_count = 原 + 1`），复制术式/眼别/方案/患者等全部要素；6) 原预约置 `POSTPONED`；7) 写 `m11_reservation_change`（`change_type = 'POSTPONED'`，`new_reservation_no` 回填，`delay_days` 计算）；8) 推送 M06 取消原台次并推送新申请；9) 若需审批（24 小时内）则校验审批单号；10) 通知患者；11) 支持 `Idempotency-Key`。

**错误码：** `"1105009"`、`"1105034"`、`"1105035"`、`"1105036"`、`"1105033"`、`"1105021"`、`"1105020"`。

#### API-31 标记未到

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005/no-show
```

```json
{
  "reason_detail": "手术日多次电话联系未果，患者未到院，家属称其已去外地",
  "contact_attempts": 3,
  "version": 4
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "old_status": "READY",
    "new_status": "NO_SHOW",
    "change_no": "BG202610310003",
    "no_show_at": "2026-11-01T10:30:00",
    "contact_attempts": 3,
    "followup_recall_generated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态在 `CONFIRMED`/`PREPARING`/`READY`（已开台不能标未到）→ `"1105009"`；2) 校验手术日 ≤ 今天（不能提前标未到）；3) 校验无未缴费用异常（`payment_status_snap` 若有欠费则提示 → `"1105038"`）；4) 状态置 `NO_SHOW`，写 `no_show_at`；5) 写 `m11_reservation_change`（`change_type = 'NO_SHOW'`）；6) 推送 M06 取消台次释放；7) 生成召回任务（供客服跟进）；8) 写状态日志 `NO_SHOW`。

**错误码：** `"1105009"`、`"1105038"`、`"1105019"`。

#### API-32 变更记录

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005/changes
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "items": [
      {
        "change_no": "BG202610310001",
        "change_seq": 1,
        "change_type": "POSTPONE",
        "original_surgery_date": "2026-11-01",
        "new_surgery_date": "2026-11-05",
        "new_reservation_no": "YY202610310012",
        "delay_days": 4,
        "reason_code": "PATIENT_REQUEST",
        "reason_detail": "患者临时出差，申请延后一周",
        "is_hospital_side": false,
        "approval_status": null,
        "refund_amount_snap": null,
        "patient_notified": true,
        "created_at": "2026-10-31T17:30:00"
      }
    ],

    "change_count": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `reservation_id` 查 `m11_reservation_change`，按 `change_seq` 升序；2) 金额字段为 `_snap` 快照原样返回；3) `change_count` 与主表 `change_count` 一致（不一致则告警）。**错误码：** `"1105001"`。

#### API-33 取手术预约（内部契约 P-07）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/reservations/YY202610310005
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "张先生",
    "refractive_type": "SMILE",
    "eye_side": "OS",
    "laterality_mark": "OS",
    "surgery_date": "2026-11-01",
    "surgeon_id": 2001,
    "plan_no": "FA202610310021",
    "status": "READY",
    "consent_completed": true,
    "checkin_completed": true,
    "eye_side_verified": true,
    "can_proceed": true,
    "block_reasons": [],
    "admission_result_snap": "PASS",
    "surgery_status_snap": "READY",
    "query_time": "2026-11-01T08:25:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `reservation_no` 查主表 → `"1105001"`；2) 计算 `can_proceed`：四项准备全部完成且最近核对 `eye_side_match = true` 且状态为 `READY`/`IN_SURGERY`；3) `block_reasons` 列出不可进入手术的原因（供 M06/M09 展示）；4) 结果缓存 Redis `m11:reservation:brief:{no}` TTL 60 秒，变更时主动失效；5) **SLA：P95 ≤ 100ms**。

**错误码：** `"1105001"`。

#### API-34 患者手术预约清单（内部契约 P-08）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/reservations/by-patient/10021?limit=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "items": [
      {
        "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
        "surgery_date": "2026-11-01",
        "refractive_type": "SMILE",
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "status": "READY",
        "surgeon_name": "王医师",
        "followup_plan_no": null
      },
      {
        "reservation_no": "YY202509100011",
        "surgery_date": "2025-09-10",
        "refractive_type": "FS_LASIK",
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "status": "SURGERY_DONE",
        "surgeon_name": "王医师",
        "followup_plan_no": "SF202509100002"
      }
    ],
    "total": 2
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `patient_id` 查预约，走 `idx_m11_reservation_patient`，按 `surgery_date DESC`；2) `limit` 缺省 20，上限 100；3) 提供历史与当前预约，供 M07 医生工作站展示「该患者做过的屈光手术」；4) **SLA：P95 ≤ 120ms**。

**错误码：** `40001`。

#### API-35 手术完成回调（内部契约 P-09）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m11/reservations/on-surgery-completed
Idempotency-Key: 4b8e2c9f-6a1d-4f3b-9e7c-2d5a8b1f4c7e
```

```json
{
  "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
  "surgery_completed_at": "2026-11-01T08:42:00",
  "surgery_outcome": "COMPLETED",
  "actual_start_time": "2026-11-01T08:31:00",
  "actual_end_time": "2026-11-01T08:42:00",
  "m09_surgery_id": 77001,
  "surgeon_id": 2001,
  "eye_side_actual": "OS",
  "intraop_summary": {
    "laser_type": "FEMTOSECOND",
    "ablation_depth_um": 108,
    "optical_zone_mm": 6.5,
    "suction_time_sec": 22
  },
  "blood_loss_ml": 0,
  "complications": [],
  "caller": "M09"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "accepted": true,
    "idempotent": false,
    "old_status": "READY",
    "new_status": "SURGERY_DONE",
    "followup_plan_no": "SF202611010003",
    "followup_generated": true,
    "eye_side_matched": true,
    "eye_side_warning": null,
    "processed_at": "2026-11-01T08:43:10"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**响应（重复回调，幂等命中）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "accepted": true,
    "idempotent": true,
    "old_status": "SURGERY_DONE",
    "new_status": "SURGERY_DONE",
    "followup_plan_no": "SF202611010003",
    "followup_generated": true,
    "eye_side_matched": true,
    "eye_side_warning": null,
    "processed_at": "2026-11-01T08:45:22"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**响应（实际眼别与预约不一致 → 告警但不拒收）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "reservation_no": "YY202610310005",
    "accepted": true,
    "idempotent": false,
    "old_status": "READY",
    "new_status": "SURGERY_DONE",
    "followup_plan_no": "SF202611010003",
    "followup_generated": true,
    "eye_side_matched": false,
    "eye_side_warning": "严重告警：实际手术眼别 OD 与预约眼别 OS 不一致，已上报质控与不良事件",
    "processed_at": "2026-11-01T08:43:10"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑（核心接口）：**

1. **幂等检查**：先查 Redis `m11:idem:reservation:p09:{Idempotency-Key}`；命中则直接返回缓存结果（`idempotent = true`）。再查数据库 `followup_generated = true` 且 `status = 'SURGERY_DONE'` 的预约，若已是终态则返回 `idempotent = true`。
2. 按 `reservation_no` 查预约 → `"1105001"`；状态必须是 `READY`/`IN_SURGERY`（允许 `READY` 直接跳到术毕以容错）→ `"1105009"`。
3. **眼别比对**：若 `eye_side_actual` 存在且与 `reservation.eye_side` 不一致 → **不拒收**（手术已完成，事实必须记录），但：
   - 置 `eye_side_matched = false`；
   - 返回 `eye_side_warning` 高优先级告警文本；
   - 写 `EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH` 安全审计日志（`is_safety_related = TRUE`, `result = 'FAILED'`）；
   - 推送告警至质控、科主任、不良事件上报（M11-07/M02）。
4. 更新主表：`actual_start_time`、`actual_end_time`、`surgery_completed_at`、`surgery_outcome_snap`、`intraop_summary_snap`、`surgery_status_snap`、状态置 `SURGERY_DONE`。
5. **触发术后随访计划生成**：调 M11-06 内部接口创建随访计划（D1/W1/M1/M3/M6/Y1），成功则回填 `followup_plan_no` 并置 `followup_generated = true`；失败则 `"1105039"` 并由 Celery 重试（不阻塞回调成功返回，标记待补偿）。
6. 写状态日志 `COMPLETE`；写审计日志 `P09_CALLBACK`。
7. 发布事件 `m11.surgery.completed`（含 `reservation_no`、`patient_id`、`followup_plan_no`）。
8. 写幂等键（TTL 24h）。
9. **SLA：P95 ≤ 150ms**（不含随访生成的异步部分）。
10. **必须支持 Idempotency-Key**（M06/M09 网络重试场景）。

**错误码：** `"1105001"`、`"1105009"`、`"1105022"`、`"1105039"`、`40001`。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m11/reservation.py
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— 路由层"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m11.reservation import (
    ChecklistExecIn, ChecklistUnblockIn, ConsentGenerateIn, ConsentRevokeIn,
    ConsentSignIn, EducationExecIn, EyeSideChangeIn, LateralityMarkIn,
    MedicationExecIn, MedicationPlanIn, MedicationSkipIn, NoShowIn,
    ReservationCancelIn, ReservationCreateIn, ReservationPostponeIn,
    ReservationQuery, ReservationSubmitIn, ReservationUpdateIn,
    SurgeryCompletedIn, WorkbenchQuery, BoardQuery,
)
from app.services.m11.reservation_service import ReservationService
from app.services.m11.reservation_prep_service import ReservationPrepService
from app.services.m11.reservation_consent_service import ConsentService
from app.services.m11.reservation_check_service import ChecklistService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m11/reservations", tags=["M11-05 手术预约"])
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m11/reservations",
                            tags=["M11-05 内部契约"])


# ==================== 工作台与看板 ====================
@router.get("/workbench/overview", summary="工作台今日概览指标")
async def workbench_overview(
    work_date: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="工作日期，缺省今天"),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """返回当日台次分布与近 30 天安全指标。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_overview(work_date, org_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/pending", summary="待办汇总")
async def workbench_pending(
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """准备未齐、同意书待签、核对待做三类待办。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_pending(org_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/plans-ready", summary="方案已审核待预约清单")
async def workbench_plans_ready(
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """从 M11-04 P-05 取方案已审未预约清单。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    items, total = await svc.plans_ready(org_id, page, page_size, user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size))


@router.get("/board", summary="手术日看板")
async def board(
    board_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    room_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """按手术间分组返回当日台次与准备完成度。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.board(board_date, room_code, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/board/statistics", summary="看板效率与安全指标")
async def board_statistics(
    board_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:stat")),
):
    """效率指标与安全指标，来自日汇总表加当日实时补充。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.board_statistics(board_date, user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 预约列表与详情 ====================
@router.get("", summary="预约分页列表")
async def list_reservations(
    surgery_date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    surgery_date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="逗号分隔多选"),
    refractive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    eye_side: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    room_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    patient_keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    pending_only: bool = Query(False),
    safety_flag: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """多条件检索预约，支持准备未齐与安全异常过滤。"""
    q = ReservationQuery(
        surgery_date_from=surgery_date_from,
        surgery_date_to=surgery_date_to,
        status=status.split(",") if status else None,
        refractive_type=refractive_type.split(",") if refractive_type else None,
        eye_side=eye_side.split(",") if eye_side else None,
        surgeon_id=surgeon_id, room_code=room_code,
        patient_keyword=patient_keyword,
        pending_only=pending_only, safety_flag=safety_flag,
        page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    items, total = await svc.list_reservations(q, user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size))


@router.get("/{reservation_no}", summary="预约详情")
async def get_reservation(
    reservation_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """预约详情，含眼别一致性与准备完成度。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(reservation_no, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/{reservation_no}/status-timeline", summary="状态流转时间轴")
async def status_timeline(
    reservation_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:view")),
):
    """状态轨迹与各段停留时长。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)
    data = await svc.status_timeline(reservation_no, user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 预约登记与流转 ====================
@router.post("", summary="新建预约（草稿）")
async def create_reservation(
    payload: ReservationCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:reservation:create")),
):
    """校验方案已审、准入 PASS、医师授权后生成 YY 号草稿。"""
    svc = ReservationService(db)

```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m11/reservation_check_service.py
"""M11-05 术前核对服务 —— 眼别安全闸门（本子模块最关键的服务）"""

import json
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.reservation import M11Reservation
from app.models.m11.reservation_detail import (
    M11Consent, M11ReservationChecklist,
)
from app.models.m11.reservation_track import (
    M11ReservationLog, M11ReservationStatusLog,
)
from app.schemas.m11.reservation import ChecklistExecIn

SEVEN_KEYS = ["name", "gender", "age", "id_card", "operation", "eye_side",
              "anesthesia"]
REQUIRED_ROUNDS = ["WARD", "OR_ENTRY", "TIME_OUT"]
REQUIRED_VERIFY_FLAGS = ["identity_verified", "consent_verified",
                         "medication_verified", "education_verified",
                         "laterality_mark_verified"]


class ChecklistService:
    """术前核对服务：三查七对 + 眼别四处比对 + 强制硬阻断。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        """初始化。"""
        self.db = db

    async def _get_reservation(self, reservation_no: str) -> M11Reservation:
        """取预约，不存在抛 1105001。"""
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation).where(
                M11Reservation.reservation_no == reservation_no,
                M11Reservation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if row is None:
            raise BizException("1105001", "手术预约不存在或已终止")
        return row

    async def _get_consent_eye_side(self, reservation_id: int) -> Optional[str]:
        """取该预约最近一份已签署手术同意书中确认的眼别。"""
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11Consent.eye_side_confirmed).where(
                M11Consent.reservation_id == reservation_id,
                M11Consent.consent_type == "SURGERY",
                M11Consent.sign_status == "SIGNED",
                M11Consent.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .order_by(M11Consent.patient_sign_at.desc())
        )).scalars().first()
        return row

    def judge_eye_side(self, reservation_eye: str,
                       plan_eye: Optional[str],
                       consent_eye: Optional[str],
                       verified_eye: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """眼别四处比对（详细设计 3.4（4）算法）。

        规则：
          1) 实际核实眼别必须等于预约眼别（最硬一条）；
          2) 方案眼别若存在，必须等于预约眼别；
          3) 同意书眼别若存在，必须等于预约眼别。
        """
        mismatched: List[str] = []

        if verified_eye is None:
            match = None
        else:
            match = True
            if verified_eye != reservation_eye:
                mismatched.append("reservation")
            if plan_eye is not None and plan_eye != reservation_eye:
                mismatched.append("plan")
            if consent_eye is not None and consent_eye != reservation_eye:
                mismatched.append("consent")
            match = len(mismatched) == 0

        return {
            "reservation": reservation_eye,
            "plan": plan_eye,
            "consent": consent_eye,
            "verified": verified_eye,
            "match": match,
            "mismatched_sources": mismatched,
            "detected_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        }

    async def exec_checklist(self, reservation_no: str,
                             payload: ChecklistExecIn,
                             operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行术前核对：眼别不一致时强制硬阻断。"""
        resv = await self._get_reservation(reservation_no)
        if resv.status not in ("CONFIRMED", "PREPARING", "READY"):
            raise BizException("1105009",
                               "预约状态不允许术前核对（当前 %s）" % resv.status)

        consent_eye = await self._get_consent_eye_side(resv.id)

        # ---------- 第一步：眼别四处比对（最优先） ----------
        eye = self.judge_eye_side(
            reservation_eye=resv.eye_side,
            plan_eye=resv.eye_side_plan_snap,
            consent_eye=consent_eye,
            verified_eye=payload.eye_side_verified,
        )

        check_seq = await self._next_check_seq(resv.id, payload.check_round)

        # ---------- 第二步：眼别不一致 → 强制硬阻断，立即返回 ----------
        if eye["match"] is False:
            record = await self._save_checklist(
                resv, payload, check_seq, "BLOCKED", eye,
                seven_all=False,
                blocked_reason=(
                    "眼别四处不一致：预约 %s / 方案 %s / 同意书 %s / 实核 %s"
                    % (eye["reservation"], eye["plan"], eye["consent"],
                       eye["verified"])
                ),
                operator=operator,
            )
            # 预约状态锁定：若已 READY 则回退 PREPARING
            if resv.status == "READY":
                await self._transition(resv, "PREPARING", "CHECKLIST_BLOCK",
                                       operator, reason="眼别不一致阻断，退回准备中",
                                       source="USER")
            await self._alert_eye_mismatch(resv, record, eye)
            await self._write_log(
                resv, "EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK", "SAFETY",
                summary="眼别不一致，核对硬阻断", is_safety=True,
                operator=operator, result="FAILED", error_code="1105012")
            await self.db.commit()
            raise BizException(
                "1105012", "眼别不一致，核对已强制阻断",
                data={
                    "reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
                    "check_id": record.id,
                    "check_result": "BLOCKED",
                    "eye_side": eye,
                    "blocked_reason": record.blocked_reason,
                    "alerted_to": ["surgeon", "director", "quality"],
                    "reservation_locked": True,
                })

        # ---------- 第三步：七对与必备核实项 ----------
        seven_all = all(bool(payload.seven_items.get(k)) for k in SEVEN_KEYS)
        failed_items = [k for k in SEVEN_KEYS if not payload.seven_items.get(k)]
        verify_ok = all(getattr(payload, f) for f in REQUIRED_VERIFY_FLAGS)

        round_extra_ok = True
        round_extra_msg = ""
        if payload.check_round == "TIME_OUT":
            if not payload.surgeon_present:
                round_extra_ok = False
                round_extra_msg = "Time-Out 核对必须主刀医师在场"
            elif not payload.participant_ids or len(payload.participant_ids) < 3:
                round_extra_ok = False
                round_extra_msg = "Time-Out 核对必须至少 3 名在场人员共同确认"
        elif payload.check_round == "OR_ENTRY":
            if not payload.handover_to_id:
                round_extra_ok = False
                round_extra_msg = "手术室入口交接必须指定接班人"

        if not (seven_all and verify_ok and round_extra_ok):
            result = "FAILED"
            reason = round_extra_msg or (
                "七对未全部通过：%s" % ",".join(failed_items)
                if failed_items else "必备核实项未完成")
            record = await self._save_checklist(
                resv, payload, check_seq, result, eye,
                seven_all=seven_all,
                failed_items=",".join(failed_items) if failed_items else None,
                blocked_reason=None, operator=operator)
            await self.db.commit()
            raise BizException("1105013",
                               "术前核对未通过：%s" % reason,
                               data={"check_id": record.id,
                                     "failed_items": failed_items,
                                     "round_extra_ok": round_extra_ok})

        # ---------- 第四步：全部通过 ----------
        record = await self._save_checklist(
            resv, payload, check_seq, "PASSED", eye,
            seven_all=seven_all, blocked_reason=None, operator=operator)

        all_passed = await self._all_rounds_passed(resv.id)
        if all_passed:
            resv.checkin_completed = True
            resv.checkin_completed_at = datetime.now()

        transitioned = await self._maybe_ready(resv, operator)
        await self._write_log(resv, "CHECKLIST_PASS", "SAFETY",
                              summary="%s 核对通过" % payload.check_round,
                              is_safety=True, operator=operator)
        await self.db.commit()

        progress = sum([
            bool(resv.consent_completed),
            bool(resv.preop_med_completed),
            bool(resv.education_completed),
            bool(resv.checkin_completed),
        ])
        return {
            "reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
            "check_id": record.id,
            "check_round": payload.check_round,
            "check_result": "PASSED",
            "eye_side": eye,
            "seven_items_all_passed": True,
            "reservation_status_transitioned": transitioned,
            "new_status": resv.status,
            "ready_progress": progress,
        }

    async def _next_check_seq(self, reservation_id: int, round_: str) -> int:
        """同轮次已有记录则序号 +1。"""
        cnt = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(M11ReservationChecklist).where(
                M11ReservationChecklist.reservation_id == reservation_id,
                M11ReservationChecklist.check_round == round_,
                M11ReservationChecklist.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar() or 0
        return cnt + 1

    async def _save_checklist(self, resv: M11Reservation,
                              payload: ChecklistExecIn, check_seq: int,
                              result: str, eye: Dict[str, Any],
                              seven_all: bool,
                              failed_items: Optional[str] = None,
                              blocked_reason: Optional[str] = None,
                              operator: Any = None
                              ) -> M11ReservationChecklist:
        """写入核对记录（含眼别四处快照）。"""
        now = datetime.now()
        record = M11ReservationChecklist(
            reservation_id=resv.id,
            reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
            patient_id=resv.patient_id,
            check_round=payload.check_round,
            check_seq=check_seq,
            check_time=now,
            check_result=result,
            # 眼别四处快照
            eye_side_reservation=eye["reservation"],
            eye_side_plan=eye["plan"],
            eye_side_consent=eye["consent"],
            eye_side_verified=eye["verified"],
            eye_side_match=eye["match"],
            eye_side_mismatch_detail=(eye if eye["match"] is False else None),
            laterality_mark_verified=payload.laterality_mark_verified,
            # 身份与七对
            identity_verified=payload.identity_method is not None,
            identity_method=payload.identity_method,
            wristband_no=payload.wristband_no,
            patient_name_confirmed=payload.patient_name_confirmed,
            patient_birth_confirmed=payload.patient_birth_confirmed,
            check_seven_items=payload.seven_items,
            seven_items_all_passed=seven_all,
            failed_items=failed_items,
            # 其他核实项
            consent_verified=payload.consent_verified,
            medication_verified=payload.medication_verified,
            education_verified=payload.education_verified,
            fasting_verified=payload.fasting_verified,
            lab_result_verified=payload.lab_result_verified,
            implant_verified=payload.implant_verified,
            equipment_qualified=payload.equipment_qualified,
            preop_photo_taken=payload.preop_photo_taken,
            jewelry_removed=payload.jewelry_removed,
            makeup_removed=payload.makeup_removed,
            # 交接
            handover_to_id=payload.handover_to_id,
            handover_time=now if payload.handover_to_id else None,
            handover_from_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            handover_from_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            # 执行人与参与人
            executor_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            executor_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            executor_role=getattr(operator, "role", None),
            participant_ids=payload.participant_ids,
            participant_count=(len(payload.participant_ids)
                               if payload.participant_ids else None),
            surgeon_present=payload.surgeon_present,
            patient_response_ok=payload.patient_response_ok,
            # 硬阻断
            blocked_reason=blocked_reason,
            blocked_at=now if result == "BLOCKED" else None,
            blocked_by=(getattr(operator, "id", None)
                        if result == "BLOCKED" else None),
            signature_url=payload.signature_url,
            photo_url=payload.photo_url,
            remark=payload.remark,
            org_id=resv.org_id,
        )
        self.db.add(record)
        await self.db.flush()
        return record

    async def _all_rounds_passed(self, reservation_id: int) -> bool:
        """三轮核对是否全部通过（以每轮最新一条为准）。"""
        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ReservationChecklist.check_round,
                   M11ReservationChecklist.check_result).where(
                M11ReservationChecklist.reservation_id == reservation_id,
                M11ReservationChecklist.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).all()
        latest: Dict[str, str] = {}
        for round_, result in rows:
            latest[round_] = result
        return all(latest.get(r) == "PASSED" for r in REQUIRED_ROUNDS)

    async def _maybe_ready(self, resv: M11Reservation,
                           operator: Any) -> bool:
        """四项准备齐备且最近核对眼别一致时，自动置 READY。"""
        if not (resv.consent_completed and resv.preop_med_completed
                and resv.education_completed and resv.checkin_completed):
            return False
        latest_match = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ReservationChecklist.eye_side_match)
            .where(M11ReservationChecklist.reservation_id == resv.id,
                   M11ReservationChecklist.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .order_by(M11ReservationChecklist.check_time.desc())
        )).scalars().first()
        if latest_match is not True:
            return False
        if resv.status == "PREPARING":
            await self._transition(resv, "READY", "PREP_COMPLETE", operator,
                                   reason="四项准备齐备且眼别核对通过",
                                   source="USER")
            resv.ready_at = datetime.now()
            # 记录准时性
            return True
        return False

    async def _transition(self, resv: M11Reservation, to_status: str,
                          action: str, operator: Any, reason: str,
                          source: str = "USER") -> None:
        """统一状态流转：更新主表并写状态日志（含停留时长）。"""
        from_status = resv.status
        prev_at = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ReservationStatusLog.operated_at)
            .where(M11ReservationStatusLog.reservation_id == resv.id)
            .order_by(M11ReservationStatusLog.operated_at.desc())
        )).scalars().first()
        now = datetime.now()
        dwell = int((now - prev_at).total_seconds()) if prev_at else None

        resv.status = to_status
        self.db.add(M11ReservationStatusLog(
            reservation_id=resv.id,
            reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
            patient_id=resv.patient_id,
            from_status=from_status,
            to_status=to_status,
            transition_action=action,
            transition_source=source,
            dwell_seconds=dwell,
            dwell_level=self._dwell_level(dwell),
            status_reason=reason,
            eye_side_snapshot=resv.eye_side,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            operator_role=getattr(operator, "role", None),
            operated_at=now,
            org_id=resv.org_id,
        ))

    def _dwell_level(self, seconds: Optional[int]) -> Optional[str]:
        """停留时长分级（阈值见系统参数）。"""
        if seconds is None:
            return None
        if seconds < 24 * 3600:
            return "FAST"
        if seconds < 48 * 3600:
            return "SLOW"
        return "STUCK"

    async def _alert_eye_mismatch(self, resv: M11Reservation,
                                  record: M11ReservationChecklist,
                                  eye: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """眼别不一致的高优先级告警（主刀 / 科主任 / 质控）。"""
        payload = {
            "event_type": "m11.reservation.eye_mismatch_block",
            "reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
            "patient_id": resv.patient_id,
            "surgeon_id": resv.surgeon_id,
            "check_id": record.id,
            "eye_side": eye,
            "severity": "CRITICAL",
            "occurred_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        }
        await redis_client.publish("m11:alert:eye_mismatch",
                                   json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        # 站内消息 + 短信（M03-05），走 Celery 异步
        from app.tasks.m11.reservation_tasks import alert_eye_mismatch_task
        alert_eye_mismatch_task.delay(resv.reservation_no, record.id)

    async def _write_log(self, resv: M11Reservation, action: str,
                         group: str, summary: str, is_safety: bool = False,
                         operator: Any = None, result: str = "SUCCESS",
                         error_code: Optional[str] = None,
                         error_msg: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """写操作审计日志（只追加）。"""
        log_no = "LOG%s%08d" % (
            datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"),
            await redis_client.incr("m11:code:LOG"))
        self.db.add(M11ReservationLog(
            log_no=log_no,
            reservation_id=resv.id,
            reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
            patient_id=resv.patient_id,
            patient_name_mask=(resv.patient_name[:1] + "*"
                               if resv.patient_name else None),
            action=action,
            action_group=group,
            change_summary=summary,
            is_safety_related=is_safety,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            operator_role=getattr(operator, "role", None),
            result=result,
            error_code=error_code,
            error_msg=error_msg,
            operated_at=datetime.now(),
        ))

    async def unblock(self, reservation_no: str, check_id: int,
                      payload: Any, operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """解除硬阻断：必须审批单号 + 最终眼别，解除后须重新核对。"""
        resv = await self._get_reservation(reservation_no)
        record = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ReservationChecklist).where(
                M11ReservationChecklist.id == check_id,
                M11ReservationChecklist.reservation_id == resv.id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if record is None or record.check_result != "BLOCKED":
            raise BizException("1105009", "该核对记录不存在或不是硬阻断状态")
        if record.unblock_at is not None:
            raise BizException("1105009", "该阻断已解除，请勿重复操作")
        if not payload.unblock_approval_no:
            raise BizException("1105032",
                               "解除眼别阻断必须填写审批单号与最终眼别")

        # 最终眼别若与预约不同，同步更新预约眼别
        eye_changed = payload.confirmed_eye_side != resv.eye_side
        if eye_changed:
            resv.eye_side = payload.confirmed_eye_side

        record.unblock_approval_no = payload.unblock_approval_no
        record.unblock_reason = payload.unblock_reason
        record.unblock_by = getattr(operator, "id", None)
        record.unblock_at = datetime.now()

        # 眼别变更连带处理：撤销同意书 + 清空标记 + 重新核对
        consent_revoked = False
        if eye_changed:
            consents = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M11Consent).where(
                    M11Consent.reservation_id == resv.id,
                    M11Consent.sign_status.in_(["PENDING", "SIGNING", "SIGNED"]))
            )).scalars().all()
            for c in consents:
                c.sign_status = "REVOKED"
                c.revoke_reason = "眼别变更，原同意书失效"
                c.revoke_reason_code = "PLAN_CHANGED"
                c.revoked_by = getattr(operator, "id", None)
                c.revoked_at = datetime.now()
                consent_revoked = True
            resv.laterality_mark = None
            resv.laterality_marked_at = None
            resv.laterality_marked_by = None
            resv.laterality_witness_id = None
            resv.consent_completed = False
            resv.checkin_completed = False
            if resv.status == "READY":
                resv.status = "PREPARING"

        # 生成新一轮待核对的记录
        next_seq = await self._next_check_seq(resv.id, record.check_round)
        self.db.add(M11ReservationChecklist(
            reservation_id=resv.id, reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
            patient_id=resv.patient_id, check_round=record.check_round,
            check_seq=next_seq, check_result="PENDING",
            eye_side_reservation=resv.eye_side,
            eye_side_plan=resv.eye_side_plan_snap,
            org_id=resv.org_id))

        await self._write_log(
            resv, "CHECKLIST_UNBLOCK", "SAFETY",
            summary="解除眼别硬阻断，确认最终眼别 %s（审批 %s）"
                    % (payload.confirmed_eye_side, payload.unblock_approval_no),
            is_safety=True, operator=operator)
        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
            "check_id": record.id,
            "unblocked": True,
            "unblocked_at": record.unblock_at,
            "confirmed_eye_side": payload.confirmed_eye_side,
            "reservation_eye_side_updated": eye_changed,
            "new_reservation_eye_side": resv.eye_side,
            "consent_revoked": consent_revoked,
            "recheck_required": True,
            "reservation_status": resv.status,
        }
```

```python
# app/services/m11/reservation_service.py（节选）
"""M11-05 手术预约主服务 —— P-09 术毕回调与随访触发"""

import json
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m11.postop_client import PostopClient
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.reservation import M11Reservation
from app.schemas.m11.reservation import SurgeryCompletedIn

IDEM_PREFIX = "m11:idem:reservation:p09:"


class ReservationService:
    """手术预约服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        """初始化。"""
        self.db = db
        self.postop = PostopClient()

    async def on_surgery_completed(self, payload: SurgeryCompletedIn,
                                   idem_key: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-09 术毕回调：落快照 + 眼别比对 + 触发随访计划生成（幂等）。"""
        # ---------- 步骤 1：Redis 幂等检查 ----------
        cache_key = IDEM_PREFIX + (idem_key or "")
        if idem_key:
            cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
            if cached:
                data = json.loads(cached)
                data["idempotent"] = True
                return data

        resv = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation).where(
                M11Reservation.reservation_no == payload.reservation_no,
                M11Reservation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if resv is None:
            raise BizException("1105001", "手术预约不存在或已终止")

        # ---------- 步骤 2：数据库幂等检查（已术毕则直接返回） ----------
        if resv.status == "SURGERY_DONE" and resv.followup_generated:
            data = self._surgery_completed_out(resv, idempotent=True)
            await self._cache_idem(cache_key, data, idem_key)
            return data

        if resv.status not in ("READY", "IN_SURGERY"):
            raise BizException(
                "1105009",
                "预约状态 %s 不允许术毕回调" % resv.status)

        # ---------- 步骤 3：眼别比对（不拒收，但必须告警） ----------
        eye_matched: Optional[bool] = None
        eye_warning: Optional[str] = None
        if payload.eye_side_actual is not None:
            eye_matched = payload.eye_side_actual == resv.eye_side
            if not eye_matched:
                eye_warning = (
                    "严重告警：实际手术眼别 %s 与预约眼别 %s 不一致，"
                    "已上报质控与不良事件"
                    % (payload.eye_side_actual, resv.eye_side))
                await redis_client.publish(
                    "m11:alert:eye_mismatch_postop",
                    json.dumps({"reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
                                "reserved": resv.eye_side,
                                "actual": payload.eye_side_actual},
                               ensure_ascii=False))

        # ---------- 步骤 4：落术毕快照（术中数据只读存储，M11 不产生） ----------
        old_status = resv.status
        resv.status = "SURGERY_DONE"
        resv.actual_start_time = payload.actual_start_time
        resv.actual_end_time = payload.actual_end_time
        resv.surgery_completed_at = payload.surgery_completed_at
        resv.surgery_outcome_snap = payload.surgery_outcome
        resv.intraop_summary_snap = payload.intraop_summary
        resv.surgery_status_snap = "COMPLETED"
        resv.surgery_status_snap_at = datetime.now()
        resv.m09_reception_id = payload.m09_surgery_id

        # ---------- 步骤 5：触发术后随访计划生成 ----------
        followup_no: Optional[str] = None
        followup_ok = False
        try:
            followup_no = await self.postop.create_followup_plan(
                reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
                patient_id=resv.patient_id,
                refractive_type=resv.refractive_type,
                eye_side=resv.eye_side,
                surgery_date=resv.surgery_date,
                surgery_completed_at=payload.surgery_completed_at,
            )
            followup_ok = bool(followup_no)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            followup_ok = False

        resv.followup_plan_no = followup_no
        resv.followup_generated = followup_ok
        await self.db.commit()

        if not followup_ok:
            # 随访生成失败：预约状态仍为术毕，由定时任务补偿重试
            raise BizException(
                "1105039", "术后随访计划生成失败，已进入补偿队列",
                data={"reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
                      "new_status": resv.status,
                      "followup_generated": False})

        data = self._surgery_completed_out(resv, idempotent=False)
        data["old_status"] = old_status
        data["eye_side_matched"] = eye_matched
        data["eye_side_warning"] = eye_warning
        await self._cache_idem(cache_key, data, idem_key)
        return data

    def _surgery_completed_out(self, resv: M11Reservation,
                               idempotent: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """组装 P-09 响应。"""
        return {
            "reservation_no": resv.reservation_no,
            "accepted": True,
            "idempotent": idempotent,
            "old_status": None,
            "new_status": resv.status,
            "followup_plan_no": resv.followup_plan_no,
            "followup_generated": resv.followup_generated,
            "eye_side_matched": None,
            "eye_side_warning": None,
            "processed_at": datetime.now(),
        }

    async def _cache_idem(self, cache_key: str, data: Dict[str, Any],
                          idem_key: Optional[str]) -> None:
        """缓存幂等结果 24 小时。"""
        if idem_key and cache_key.endswith(idem_key):
            await redis_client.setex(cache_key, 86400,
                                     json.dumps(data, default=str))
```

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m11:reservation:detail:{no}` | String(JSON) | 120s | 预约详情（页面用） | 预约变更/状态流转 |
| `m11:reservation:brief:{no}` | String(JSON) | 60s | **P-07 内部契约响应** | 预约变更 |
| `m11:reservation:board:{date}:{room}` | String(JSON) | 30s | 手术日看板数据 | 状态变化、定时刷新 |
| `m11:reservation:workbench:{org}:{date}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 工作台概览指标 | 定时刷新 |
| `m11:reservation:board_stat:{date}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 看板效率与安全指标 | 日汇总刷新 |
| `m11:reservation:eye_side:{no}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 眼别四处比对结果（核对前预取） | 同意书签署/眼别变更 |
| `m11:reservation:consent:{consent_no}` | String(JSON) | 600s | 同意书详情（不含正文） | 签署/撤销/校验 |
| `m11:reservation:checklist:{no}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 核对记录汇总 | 核对执行 |
| `m11:idem:reservation:create:{key}` | String | 24h | 新建预约幂等键 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:idem:reservation:p09:{key}` | String | 24h | **P-09 术毕回调幂等键** | 自然过期 |
| `m11:idem:reservation:postpone:{key}` | String | 24h | 改期幂等键 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:idem:reservation:consent:{key}` | String | 24h | 生成同意书幂等键 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:code:YY:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(计数器) | 48h | 预约号日流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:code:TYS:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(计数器) | 48h | 同意书编号日流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:code:BG:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(计数器) | 48h | 停台改期单号日流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:code:LOG:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(计数器) | 48h | 日志流水日流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m11:lock:reservation:{no}` | String | 60s | 预约编辑互斥锁 | 主动释放 |
| `m11:lock:checklist:{no}` | String | 30s | 核对执行互斥锁（防并发重复核对） | 主动释放 |
| `m11:alert:eye_mismatch` | Pub/Sub 频道 | — | **眼别不一致实时告警广播** | — |

**容量估算：**

| 数据 | 单条大小 | 数量 | 内存 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 预约详情缓存 | 4 KB | 500 个活跃预约 | 2 MB |
| 预约简要缓存（P-07） | 1.5 KB | 1,000 个 | 1.5 MB |
| 看板数据 | 12 KB | 10 个（日期×手术间） | 0.12 MB |
| 工作台概览 | 3 KB | 5 个 | 0.02 MB |
| 同意书缓存 | 8 KB | 200 个 | 1.6 MB |
| 幂等键 | 0.8 KB | 3,000 条 | 2.4 MB |
| 计数器与锁 | 0.1 KB | 200 个 | 0.02 MB |
| **合计** | — | — | **约 7.7 MB** |

> **设计说明：** 本子模块缓存体量很小（< 10 MB），这是因为核心数据是「预约」——日均仅 15 条，且读多写少。缓存的主要目的不是省 DB 查询，而是**支撑 P-07 的 100ms SLA**（M06/M09/M16-04 高频调用）与**看板的 30 秒刷新**。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m11/reservation_tasks.py
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— Celery 异步任务"""

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from celery import shared_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=3, default_retry_delay=60)
def push_m06_apply_task(self: Any, reservation_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """推送 M06 日间手术申请（失败指数退避重试 3 次）。"""
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_push_m06_apply(reservation_no))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("push_m06_apply retry: %s", exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (2 ** self.request.retries))


async def _push_m06_apply(reservation_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """实际推送逻辑：调 M06 E-06-01 API-32。"""
    from app.services.m11.m06_client_service import M06ClientService
    svc = M06ClientService()
    return await svc.push_apply(reservation_no)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=3, default_retry_delay=120)
def push_m06_cancel_task(self: Any, reservation_no: str,
                         change_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """推送 M06 取消申请（停台/改期时释放台次）。"""
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_push_m06_cancel(reservation_no, change_no))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("push_m06_cancel retry: %s", exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=120 * (2 ** self.request.retries))


async def _push_m06_cancel(reservation_no: str, change_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """调 M06 取消接口并回写 m06_cancel_pushed。"""
    from app.services.m11.m06_client_service import M06ClientService
    svc = M06ClientService()
    return await svc.push_cancel(reservation_no, change_no)


@shared_task
def alert_eye_mismatch_task(reservation_no: str, check_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """眼别不一致高优先级告警：站内消息 + 短信 + 不良事件预登记。"""
    return asyncio.run(_alert_eye_mismatch(reservation_no, check_id))


async def _alert_eye_mismatch(reservation_no: str,
                              check_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """并发通知主刀、科主任、质控；并写一条不良事件预登记。"""
    from sqlalchemy import select

    from app.clients.m03_client import M03Client
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m11.reservation import M11Reservation
    from app.models.m11.reservation_detail import M11ReservationChecklist

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        resv = (await db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation).where(
                M11Reservation.reservation_no == reservation_no)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        record = (await db.execute(
            select(M11ReservationChecklist).where(
                M11ReservationChecklist.id == check_id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if resv is None or record is None:
            return {"skipped": True}

        eye = record.eye_side_mismatch_detail or {}
        detail = (
            "【严重安全告警】手术预约 %s：眼别不一致已被强制阻断。\n"
            "预约 %s / 方案 %s / 同意书 %s / 实核 %s\n"
            "请立即核实并走审批解除流程。"
            % (reservation_no, eye.get("reservation"), eye.get("plan"),
               eye.get("consent"), eye.get("verified")))

        m03 = M03Client()
        receivers = [resv.surgeon_id]
        await m03.send_message(
            channel="SMS", template_code="M11_EYE_MISMATCH_ALERT",
            receivers=[str(r) for r in receivers if r],
            content=detail, biz_no=reservation_no)
        return {"reservation_no": reservation_no, "alerted": True}


@shared_task
def generate_followup_retry_task(reservation_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """随访计划生成补偿任务（P-09 首次生成失败时定时重试）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_generate_followup_retry(reservation_no))


async def _generate_followup_retry(reservation_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """补偿生成随访计划并回填。"""
    from sqlalchemy import select

    from app.clients.m11.postop_client import PostopClient
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m11.reservation import M11Reservation

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        resv = (await db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation).where(
                M11Reservation.reservation_no == reservation_no,
                M11Reservation.status == "SURGERY_DONE",
                M11Reservation.followup_generated.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if resv is None:
            return {"skipped": True, "reason": "no pending followup"}

        postop = PostopClient()
        plan_no = await postop.create_followup_plan(
            reservation_no=resv.reservation_no,
            patient_id=resv.patient_id,
            refractive_type=resv.refractive_type,
            eye_side=resv.eye_side,
            surgery_date=resv.surgery_date,
            surgery_completed_at=resv.surgery_completed_at)
        resv.followup_plan_no = plan_no
        resv.followup_generated = True
        await db.commit()
        return {"reservation_no": reservation_no, "followup_plan_no": plan_no}


@shared_task
def verify_consent_tamper_task() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """每日全量同意书防篡改校验（取近 2 年仍有手术关联的记录）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_verify_consent_tamper())


async def _verify_consent_tamper() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """逐份复算内容哈希，不一致即告警。"""
    from app.services.m11.reservation_consent_service import ConsentService
    svc = ConsentService(None)
    return await svc.verify_all_recent()


@shared_task
def refresh_daily_summary_task(summary_date: str,
                               mode: str = "HOURLY") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """刷新手术日看板日汇总（DETAIL / SURGEON / ROOM / TOTAL 四层）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_refresh_daily_summary(summary_date, mode))


async def _refresh_daily_summary(summary_date: str,
                                 mode: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """按维度重算 m11_reservation_daily。"""
    from app.services.m11.reservation_daily_service import (
        ReservationDailyService,
    )
    svc = ReservationDailyService()
    return await svc.refresh(summary_date, mode)


@shared_task
def notify_changed_patient_task(reservation_no: str, notify_type: str,
                                channel: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """停台/改期后通知患者（失败重试 3 次后转人工）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_notify_changed_patient(reservation_no, notify_type,
                                               channel))


async def _notify_changed_patient(reservation_no: str, notify_type: str,
                                  channel: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """调 M03-05 发送短信/微信通知。"""
    from app.clients.m03_client import M03Client
    from app.services.m11.reservation_notify_service import (
        ReservationNotifyService,
    )
    svc = ReservationNotifyService()
    msg = await svc.build_message(reservation_no, notify_type)
    m03 = M03Client()
    msg_id = await m03.send_message(
        channel=channel, template_code="M11_RESERVATION_CHANGED",
        receivers=[msg["phone"]], content=msg["content"],
        biz_no=reservation_no)
    return {"reservation_no": reservation_no, "message_id": msg_id}
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务名 | Cron | 说明 | 影响范围 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_m11_reservation_med_miss` | `*/15 * * * *` | 扫描超期未执行的术前用药，标记 `MISSED` 并告警护士站 | `m11_reservation_medication` |
| `job_m11_reservation_consent_expire` | `0 * * * *` | 每小时扫描过期的同意书，置 `EXPIRED` 并提示重签 | `m11_consent` |
| `job_m11_reservation_no_show` | `0 6 * * *` | 手术日为昨天且未术毕的预约自动标记 `NO_SHOW` | `m11_reservation` |
| `job_m11_reservation_daily_refresh` | `5 * * * *` | 每小时刷新当日手术日汇总 | `m11_reservation_daily` |
| `job_m11_reservation_daily_nightly` | `30 1 * * *` | 夜间全量重算昨日与前日汇总（数据收敛） | `m11_reservation_daily` |
| `job_m11_reservation_followup_retry` | `*/10 * * * *` | 补偿术毕但随访未生成的预约 | `m11_reservation` |
| `job_m11_reservation_consent_tamper` | `0 2 * * *` | 每日同意书防篡改全量校验 | `m11_consent` |
| `job_m11_reservation_checklist_gate` | `*/5 * * * *` | 校验「READY 但眼别未通过」的异常数据并回退 | `m11_reservation` |
| `job_m11_reservation_m06_retry` | `*/20 * * * *` | 重试 `m06_sync_status = 'FAILED'` 的推送 | `m11_reservation` |
| `job_m11_reservation_code_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *` | 预约号/同意书号计数器与 DB max 对账 | Redis 计数器 |
| `job_m11_reservation_ref_reconcile` | `45 3 * * *` | 悬挂引用检查（患者/方案/检查/台次） | 全表 |
| `job_m11_reservation_log_partition` | `0 1 1 * *` | 预建未来 3 个月日志分区，DETACH 超 36 个月 | 两张分区表 |
| `job_m11_reservation_stat_daily_snapshot` | `10 2 * * *` | 生成质量与效率指标快照（供 M11-08/M22） | `m11_reservation_daily` |

```python
# app/jobs/m11/reservation_jobs.py
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— APScheduler 定时任务"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger
from sqlalchemy import select, text, update

from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.reservation import M11Reservation
from app.models.m11.reservation_detail import (
    M11Consent, M11ReservationMedication,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
scheduler = AsyncIOScheduler(timezone="Asia/Shanghai")


async def scan_medication_missed() -> dict:
    """超期未执行的术前用药标记为 MISSED 并告警。"""
    from app.core.config import settings

    warn_min = settings.M11_RESV_MED_MISS_WARN_MIN
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        result = await db.execute(
            text(
                """
                UPDATE m11_reservation_medication m
                SET exec_status = 'MISSED',
                    skip_reason = '系统自动标记：超过计划时间未执行',
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                WHERE m.is_deleted = FALSE
                  AND m.exec_status = 'PENDING'
                  AND m.planned_time < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - (:warn_min || ' minutes')::interval
                RETURNING m.reservation_no, m.drug_category
                """
            ),
            {"warn_min": warn_min},
        )
        rows = result.fetchall()
        await db.commit()
    if rows:
        logger.warning("marked %d medication records as MISSED", len(rows))
        await redis_client.publish(
            "m11:alert:med_missed", str(len(rows)))
    return {"missed": len(rows)}


async def expire_consents() -> dict:
    """把已过有效期的同意书置为 EXPIRED。"""
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        result = await db.execute(
            update(M11Consent)
            .where(M11Consent.sign_status == "SIGNED",
                   M11Consent.valid_to < datetime.now(),
                   M11Consent.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .values(sign_status="EXPIRED", expired_at=datetime.now())
        )
        await db.commit()
    if result.rowcount:
        logger.info("expired %d consents", result.rowcount)
    return {"expired": result.rowcount}


async def mark_no_show() -> dict:
    """手术日为昨天且未术毕的预约标记 NO_SHOW。"""
    yesterday = date.today() - timedelta(days=1)
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation).where(
                M11Reservation.surgery_date == yesterday,
                M11Reservation.status.in_(
                    ["SUBMITTED", "CONFIRMED", "PREPARING", "READY"]),
                M11Reservation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()
        for r in rows:
            r.status = "NO_SHOW"
            r.no_show_at = datetime.now()
        await db.commit()
    if rows:
        logger.info("marked %d reservations as NO_SHOW", len(rows))
    return {"no_show": len(rows)}


async def gate_ready_checklist() -> dict:
    """校验 READY 但眼别未通过的数据一致性，异常则回退 PREPARING。"""
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        result = await db.execute(
            text(
                """
                UPDATE m11_reservation r
                SET status = 'PREPARING', updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                WHERE r.is_deleted = FALSE
                  AND r.status = 'READY'
                  AND EXISTS (
                      SELECT 1 FROM m11_reservation_checklist c
                      WHERE c.reservation_id = r.id
                        AND c.is_deleted = FALSE
                        AND c.check_result = 'BLOCKED'
                        AND c.unblock_at IS NULL
                  )
                RETURNING r.reservation_no
                """
            )
        )
        rows = result.fetchall()
        await db.commit()
    if rows:
        logger.error(
            "safety gate: rolled back %d READY reservations with active block",
            len(rows))
    return {"rolled_back": len(rows)}


async def retry_m06_push() -> dict:
    """重试 M06 推送失败的预约。"""
    from app.tasks.m11.reservation_tasks import push_m06_apply_task

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M11Reservation.reservation_no).where(
                M11Reservation.m06_sync_status.in_(["FAILED", "RETRYING"]),
                M11Reservation.status == "DRAFT",
                M11Reservation.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .limit(50)
        )).scalars().all()
    for no in rows:
        push_m06_apply_task.delay(no)
    return {"retried": len(rows)}


async def ensure_log_partitions() -> dict:
    """预建未来 3 个月的日志分区。"""
    created = []
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        for table in ("m11_reservation_status_log", "m11_reservation_log"):
            for offset in range(3):
                first = (datetime.now().replace(day=1)
                         + timedelta(days=32 * (offset + 1))).replace(day=1)
                nxt = (first + timedelta(days=32)).replace(day=1)
                part = "%s_p%s" % (table, first.strftime("%Y%m"))
                try:
                    await db.execute(text(
                        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS %s PARTITION OF %s "
                        "FOR VALUES FROM ('%s') TO ('%s')"
                        % (part, table, first.isoformat(), nxt.isoformat())))
                    created.append(part)
                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                    logger.warning("create partition failed %s: %s", part, exc)
        await db.commit()
    return {"created": created}


scheduler.add_job(scan_medication_missed, CronTrigger(minute="*/15"),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_med_miss",
                  name="术前用药漏执行扫描", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(expire_consents, CronTrigger(minute=0),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_consent_expire",
                  name="同意书过期扫描", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(mark_no_show, CronTrigger(hour=6, minute=0),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_no_show",
                  name="未到自动标记", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(gate_ready_checklist, CronTrigger(minute="*/5"),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_checklist_gate",
                  name="眼别安全闸门巡检", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(retry_m06_push, CronTrigger(minute="*/20"),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_m06_retry",
                  name="M06 推送重试", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(ensure_log_partitions, CronTrigger(day=1, hour=1, minute=0),
                  id="job_m11_reservation_log_partition",
                  name="日志分区维护", replace_existing=True)
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 对象 | 规则 | 示例 | 冲突处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **手术预约号** | `YY` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位流水 | `YY202610310005` | 日流水超 9,999 报错，运维扩容 |
| **同意书编号** | `TYS` + `YYYYMMDD` + 6 位流水 | `TYS20261031000012` | 同上 |
| 停台改期单号 | `BG` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位流水 | `BG202610310001` | 同上 |
| 操作日志号 | `LOG` + `YYYYMMDD` + 8 位流水 | `LOG20261031000123` | 同上 |

**规则要点：**

1. **预约号永不复用**：改期生成新号，旧号保留在 `POSTPONED` 状态，永不重新分配。
2. **单号在 Redis 计数器生成**：`m11:code:{prefix}:{yyyyMMdd}`，48 小时后自然过期。
3. **编号生成失败不得降级为随机数**：直接抛错由调用方重试，否则破坏连续性。
4. **每日 03:00 对账**：`job_m11_reservation_code_reconcile` 比对计数器与 DB `max(no)`，差异告警。

**违反时错误码：** 号段耗尽或重复 `"50000"`。

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 校验 | 规则 | 部分索引条件 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 预约号唯一 | 全局唯一 | `reservation_no` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | `"50000"` |
| **同患者同手术日同眼别唯一** | 有效预约唯一 | `(patient_id, surgery_date, eye_side)` WHERE 状态非 `CANCELLED`/`POSTPONED`/`NO_SHOW` | `"1105004"` |
| **眼别互斥** | `OU` 与 `OD`/`OS` 不可同日共存 | 应用层校验 | `"1105005"` |
| 同意书编号唯一 | 全局唯一 | `consent_no` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | `"50000"` |
| **同预约同类型同意书唯一** | 有效同意书唯一 | `(reservation_id, consent_type)` WHERE 状态 `PENDING`/`SIGNING`/`SIGNED` | `"1105028"` |
| 核对轮次序号唯一 | 同轮次 `check_seq` 不重复 | `(reservation_id, check_round, check_seq)` | `"1105019"`（并发重试） |
| 用药记录唯一 | 同类同眼同次序唯一 | `(reservation_id, drug_seq, eye_side, dose_no)` | `"50000"` |
| 宣教类型唯一 | 同预约同类型唯一 | `(reservation_id, education_type)` | 覆盖更新（重教累加计数） |
| 变更序号唯一 | 同预约 `change_seq` 不重复 | `(reservation_id, change_seq)` | `"1105019"` |

> **关键：眼别互斥规则（`"1105005"`）**
> 数据库唯一索引含 `eye_side`，`OU` 与 `OD`/`OS` 是三个不同值，因此数据库层允许「同一天同时存在 OU 与 OD」。应用层必须额外校验：若已有 `OU` 预约或本次为 `OU`，不允许再建单眼预约（反之亦然）。这是数据库无法表达的跨行约束。

### 8.3 状态流转规则

| 当前状态 | 动作 | 目标状态 | 前置条件 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| （无） | 登记预约 | `DRAFT` | 方案已审 + 准入 PASS + 授权通过 | `"1105002"`/`"1105003"`/`"1105014"` |
| `DRAFT` | 提交推送 M06 | `SUBMITTED` | 要素齐备 + M06 推送成功 | `"1105009"`/`"1105020"` |
| `SUBMITTED` | 排台确认 | `CONFIRMED` | M06 返回台次 | `"1105009"` |
| `CONFIRMED` | 启动准备 | `PREPARING` | 手术同意书已 `SIGNED` 且未过期 | `"1105010"`/`"1105017"` |
| `PREPARING` | 准备完成 | `READY` | 四项齐备 + 最近核对眼别一致 | `"1105011"`/`"1105012"` |
| `READY` | 开始手术 | `IN_SURGERY` | M09 事件 | — |
| `IN_SURGERY` | 术毕 | `SURGERY_DONE` | P-09 回调 / M09 事件 | `"1105022"` |
| `DRAFT`~`READY` | 取消停台 | `CANCELLED` | 原因齐备（24h 内需审批） | `"1105018"`/`"1105033"` |
| `DRAFT`~`READY` | 改期 | `POSTPONED` | 新日期晚于原日期 + 次数未超限 | `"1105034"`/`"1105035"` |
| `CONFIRMED`~`READY` | 未到 | `NO_SHOW` | 手术日 ≤ 今天 | `"1105009"` |
| `SURGERY_DONE`/`CANCELLED`/`POSTPONED`/`NO_SHOW` | 任何变更 | — | **禁止（终态）** | `"1105009"` |

> 除上述主流程外，还有两条**反向规则**：
> - `PREPARING`/`READY` → `CONFIRMED`：同意书被撤销时回退（`"1105010"`）；
> - `READY` → `PREPARING`：出现眼别阻断时回退（`"1105012"`）。

### 8.4 业务校验规则

**（1）预约登记**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 方案必须已审核 | `plan.status = 'AUDITED'` | `"1105002"` |
| 准入必须 PASS | `admission_result = 'PASS'` | `"1105003"` |
| 眼别合法 | OD/OS/OU，禁 NA | `"1105012"` |
| 同患者同日同眼别无重复 | 有效预约唯一 | `"1105004"` |
| OU 与单眼互斥 | 跨行约束 | `"1105005"` |
| 手术日期 ≥ 今天 | — | `40001` |
| 提前天数 ≤ `MAX_ADVANCE_DAYS` | 90 天 | `"1105006"` |
| 主刀有术式授权 | `auth/check` 通过 | `"1105014"` |
| 单医师月量 ≤ 上限 | 120 台 | `"1105007"` |
| 单手术间日量 ≤ 上限 | 18 台 | `"1105008"` |
| 全麻/镇静需麻醉医师 | — | `"1105015"` |

**（2）术前准备**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 启动准备需同意书已签 | 手术同意书 `SIGNED` | `"1105010"` |
| 术日用药眼别必须为术眼 | 禁 OU 与非术眼 | `"1105023"` |
| 点药间隔达标 | `interval_ok` | `"1105025"` |
| 宣教理解不足须重教 | `understanding_level` | `"1105026"` |
| 宣教无法理解须监护人或翻译 | — | `"1105026"` |
| 宣教完成须患者确认 | `patient_confirm_at` | `"1105026"` |
| 同意书眼别须与预约一致 | — | `"1105012"` |
| 同意书签署须确认风险与替代方案 | — | `"1105029"` |
| 同意书有效性 | 未过期 | `"1105017"` |

**（3）术前核对与眼别安全（核心）**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| **眼别四处必须全等** | 预约/方案/同意书/实核 | `"1105012"`（硬阻断） |
| **眼别不一致必须 BLOCKED** | 不是 FAILED，必须审批解除 | `"1105012"` |
| 七对必须全部通过 | 7 项布尔全为 true | `"1105013"` |
| 必备核实项必须全勾 | 身份/同意书/用药/宣教/标记 | `"1105013"` |
| Time-Out 主刀必须在场 | — | `"1105013"` |
| Time-Out 参与人 ≥ 3 | — | `"1105013"` |
| 交接双方不得同一人 | — | `"1105013"` |
| 术眼标记须与术眼一致 | — | `"1105016"` |
| 标记见证人 ≠ 执行人 | — | `"1105012"` |
| 解除阻断须审批单号 + 最终眼别 | — | `"1105032"` |
| READY 不可存在未解除阻断 | 定时巡检回退 | `"1105012"` |

**（4）停台与改期**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 原因 ≥5 字 + 原因编码 | — | `"1105018"` |
| 24 小时内变更需审批 | — | `"1105033"` |
| 改期新日期晚于原日期 | — | `"1105034"` |
| 改期次数 ≤ 3 | — | `"1105035"` |
| 改期后检查有效期 | 超期须重做 | `"1105036"` |
| 未到须手术日 ≤ 今天 | — | `"1105009"` |
| 未到须无欠费异常 | — | `"1105038"` |

**（5）P-09 术毕回调**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 预约必须存在 | — | `"1105001"` |
| 状态须 `READY`/`IN_SURGERY` | — | `"1105009"` |
| **必须幂等** | `Idempotency-Key` + 状态检查 | `"1105040"`（幂等命中返回成功） |
| 实际眼别不一致须告警 | 不拒收但上报 | 返回 `eye_side_warning` |
| 随访计划必须生成 | 失败入补偿队列 | `"1105039"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

> ⚠️ **本子模块不计算任何金额。** 所有金额字段以 `_snap` 结尾，值为 M16 返回的快照。本节"计算"仅限时间差、完成度、比率与评分。

| 计算项 | 公式/口径 | 舍入 | 落库位置 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 准备完成度 | 四项布尔求和（0~4） | 整数 | `preop_checklist_json.ready_progress` |
| 状态停留时长 | 本状态时间 − 上一状态时间 | 整数秒 | `m11_reservation_status_log.dwell_seconds` |
| 停留分级 | <24h `FAST`；24~48h `SLOW`；≥48h `STUCK` | 枚举 | `dwell_level` |
| 点药间隔 | 本次实际 − 上次实际 | 整数分钟 | `m11_reservation_medication.interval_minutes` |
| 间隔达标 | `interval >= required − tolerance` | 布尔 | `interval_ok` |
| 眼别一致性 | 四处全等判定 | 布尔 | `m11_reservation_checklist.eye_side_match` |
| 延期天数 | 新手术日 − 原手术日 | 整数天 | `m11_reservation_change.delay_days` |
| 开台延迟 | 实际开台 − 计划开台 | 整数分钟 | 响应内计算 |
| 平均准备时长 | `AVG(ready_at − confirmed_at)`（分钟） | 2 位小数 | `m11_reservation_daily.avg_prep_minutes` |
| 平均周转时长 | `AVG(本台开台 − 上台下台)`（分钟） | 2 位小数 | `avg_turnover_minutes` |
| 平均手术时长 | `AVG(下台 − 开台)`（分钟） | 2 位小数 | `avg_surgery_minutes` |
| 完成率 | `完成 / 计划 × 100` | 2 位小数 | `completion_rate` |
| 取消率 | `取消 / 计划 × 100` | 2 位小数 | `cancel_rate` |
| 爽约率 | `未到 / 计划 × 100` | 2 位小数 | `no_show_rate` |
| 准时开台率 | `准时 / (完成 + 术中) × 100` | 2 位小数 | `on_time_rate` |
| 同意书内容哈希 | `SHA-256(正文)` 64 位小写十六进制 | — | `m11_consent.content_hash` |

**金额数据的唯一合法来源：**

| 金额 | 唯一来源 | 本模块行为 |
|---|---|---|
| 方案金额 | M16 | 存 `plan_amount_snap`，仅展示 |
| 已缴金额 | M16 | 存 `total_paid_snap`/`deposit_paid_snap`，仅展示 |
| 退款金额 | M16-08 | 存 `refund_amount_snap`，仅展示 |
| 违约金 | M16 | 存 `penalty_amount_snap`，仅展示 |

> **代码审查红线：** 本子模块任何代码路径禁止出现 `price * qty`、`amount + amount`、`amount * rate` 等金额运算。允许的仅有：`_snap` 字段的原样存取与比较（如 `total_paid_snap >= plan_amount_snap` 判断是否已缴清）。

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 对象 | 删除方式 | 约束 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 手术预约 | **不物理删除** | 只能置终态（`CANCELLED`/`POSTPONED`/`NO_SHOW`/`SURGERY_DONE`） | 被 M06/M09/M16 引用，且是质量统计原始数据 |
| 术前用药 | 逻辑删除 | `is_deleted = TRUE` | 一般不做（记录执行事实） |
| 术前宣教 | 覆盖更新 | 同类型唯一，重教计 `reeducate_count` | — |
| **知情同意书** | **永不删除** | 只能 `REVOKED`/`EXPIRED` | 法律文件，保留期与病案一致 |
| **术前核对记录** | **永不删除** | 只追加，修改须新增记录 | 医疗安全法定记录 |
| **停台改期记录** | **永不删除** | 只追加 | 质量评价原始数据 |
| 状态日志 | 只追加 | 超 36 个月归档后 `DETACH` | 不得 DELETE |
| 审计日志 | 只追加 | 超 36 个月归档后 `DETACH` | 应用账号无 UPDATE/DELETE 权限 |

**引用检查规则：**

| 场景 | 处理 |
|---|---|
| 取消预约时存在已签同意书 | 作废的是预约，同意书保留（仅置 `EXPIRED`） |
| 改期时存在已签同意书 | 若延期超有效期 → `EXPIRED`，需重签 |
| 改期时存在术中数据 | 不可能（术中状态不允许改期，`"1105009"`） |
| 眼别变更时存在已签同意书 | **强制撤销**（`REVOKED`），因确认的眼别已失效 |
| 眼别变更时存在术眼标记 | **强制清空**，需重新标记 |
| 眼别变更时存在已通过对核对 | 重置为新 `PENDING` 记录 |

### 8.7 并发控制

| 场景 | 机制 | 实现 | 冲突返回 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 预约编辑 | 乐观锁 `version` | `UPDATE ... WHERE version = :v` | `"1105019"` |
| 停台/改期 | 乐观锁 `version` | 同上 | `"1105019"` |
| 眼别变更 | 乐观锁 + 分布式锁 | `m11:lock:reservation:{no}` TTL 60s | `"1105019"` |
| **P-09 术毕回调并发** | Redis 幂等键 + 数据库状态检查 | `m11:idem:reservation:p09:{key}` + `followup_generated` 幂等 | 幂等成功（不报错） |
| **术前核对并发** | 分布式锁 + 唯一索引 | `m11:lock:checklist:{no}` TTL 30s + `(res_id, round, seq)` 唯一 | `"1105019"` |
| 同意书签署并发 | 乐观锁（`signed_count` 原子递增） | `UPDATE ... SET signed_count = signed_count + 1` | 幂等（已签则返回现状） |
| 同意书生成并发 | 部分唯一索引 | `uk_m11_consent_active` | `"1105028"` |
| 点药执行并发 | 唯一索引 + 状态条件更新 | `UPDATE ... WHERE exec_status = 'PENDING'` | 幂等（已执行直接返回） |
| **重复预约并发** | 部分唯一索引 | `uk_m11_reservation_patient_date_eye` | `"1105004"` |
| 编号生成 | Redis `INCR` 原子 | — | 天然无冲突 |

**并发控制要点：**

1. **P-09 的幂等是双保险**：Redis 幂等键处理「同一请求重试」，数据库 `followup_generated` 状态检查处理「不同 Idempotency-Key 但同一预约」的重复回调。两者缺一不可——M06 和 M09 可能同时回调同一台手术，Idempotency-Key 不同但业务意图相同。
2. **核对锁只保护同一预约的核对执行**，不锁整表。防的是「同一护士连点两次提交」与「两人同时提交不同轮次」。
3. **同意书签署用原子 `signed_count + 1`**，而非「先查后改」，避免患者与医师同时签署时计数丢失。

---

## 9. 权限与安全

### 9.1 功能权限

| 权限编码 | 说明 | 授予角色 |
|---|---|---|
| `m11:reservation:view` | 查看预约列表、详情、看板、核对记录 | 全部角色 |
| `m11:reservation:create` | 新建预约、提交推送 M06 | 预约护士、主刀医师 |
| `m11:reservation:update` | 更新预约、排台确认、停台改期、标记未到 | 预约护士、护士长 |
| `m11:reservation:prep` | 生成用药方案、执行点药、记录宣教、生成同意书 | 术前准备护士 |
| `m11:reservation:consent` | 签署同意书（患者/医师/见证人） | 护士（协助）、主刀医师、患者本人 |
| `m11:reservation:check` | 执行术前核对、记录交接 | 手术室护士 |
| `m11:reservation:sign` | 术眼标记、Time-Out 主持 | 主刀医师 |
| `m11:reservation:anesthesia` | 麻醉方式确认、禁食核查 | 麻醉医师 |
| `m11:reservation:approve` | 停台审批、眼别变更审批、解除阻断、撤销同意书 | 科主任、护士长 |
| `m11:reservation:manage` | 看板管理、排程协调、批量操作 | 手术室护士长 |
| `m11:reservation:stat` | 效率与安全指标统计 | 护士长、质控、运营 |
| `m11:reservation:audit` | 审计日志查询、同意书防篡改校验 | 质控、审计、信息科 |
| `m11:reservation:sensitive` | 查看患者手机号明文（临时授权） | 预约护士（限时） |
| `m11:reservation:export` | 导出预约数据 | 护士长、运营 |
| `m11:reservation:admin` | 数据修正、参数配置、M06 推送重试 | 信息科 |

### 9.2 权限矩阵

`●` 完全控制 `○` 可操作 `△` 只读 `—` 无权限

| 功能 | 预约护士 | 准备护士 | 手术室护士 | 主刀医师 | 麻醉医师 | 科主任 | 护士长 | 质控 | 信息科 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 预约查看 | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | △ |
| 新建预约 | ● | — | — | ○ | — | ○ | ● | — | ○ |
| 提交推送 M06 | ● | — | — | — | — | — | ● | — | ○ |
| 排台确认 | ● | — | — | — | — | — | ● | — | ○ |
| **变更眼别** | — | — | — | ○ | — | ● | — | — | ○ |
| 生成用药方案 | — | ● | — | △ | ○ | ○ | ● | — | — |
| 执行点药 | — | ● | ○ | — | — | — | ● | — | — |
| 记录宣教 | — | ● | — | △ | — | — | ● | — | — |
| 生成同意书 | — | ● | — | — | — | — | ● | — | — |
| 签署同意书 | ○ | ● | — | ● | — | — | ○ | — | — |
| 撤销同意书 | — | — | — | — | — | ● | ● | — | ○ |
| **执行术前核对** | — | ○ | ● | △ | △ | △ | ● | △ | — |
| **术眼标记** | — | — | △ | ● | — | ○ | — | — | — |
| **解除眼别阻断** | — | — | — | ○ | — | ● | ● | — | ○ |
| 停台改期 | ● | — | — | ○ | — | ● | ● | — | — |
| 审批停台 | — | — | — | ○ | — | ● | ● | — | — |

| 手术日看板 | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | △ |
